### [1] [Correlated quantum criticality and topological magnetoelectric response in Bi2Se3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03670)
*Aniekan Magnus Ukpong*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文通过将DFT与重整化的关联蜂窝模型结合研究Bi₂Se₃，发现受挫磁序与螺旋拓扑表面态由单一控制参数耦合，从而在微观层面将陈数/ℤ₂不变量、skyrmion电荷Q、轴子角θ及拓扑磁电耦合联系起来，并给出磁电能量循环的热力学界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料的核心特征是多种序参量共同支配低能物理且相互不独立。作者旨在探究Bi₂Se₃中受挫磁序与螺旋拓扑表面态如何通过统一参数耦合，并建立磁电耦合的微观机制与热力学约束。

Method: 将DFT与重整化的关联蜂窝模型结合；表面态映射到带受挫J₁–J₂交换的蜂窝Hubbard模型。对最多20个格点做严格对角化，计算Néel结构因子、价键固体序参量及动力学自旋结构因子；研究重整化紧束缚模型的能带反转、陈数与ℤ₂不变量的跳变，及其与skyrmion电荷Q的对应关系；区分轴子耦合（与skyrmion螺旋性无关）与依赖螺旋性的磁电多极耦合（环向分量按sinγ标度），并推导Q-拓扑磁电能量与循环效率的热力学界限。

Result: 在J₂/J₁≈0.22处Néel结构因子与价键固体序参量交叉，与QMC/DMRG相符；交叉点附近动力学自旋结构因子展宽，符合自旋子连续谱预期。能带反转发生在同一临界轨迹上，产生陈数与ℤ₂不变量的跳变，并由skyrmion电荷Q镜像。由此给出微观联系：Q选定轴子角θ，从而确定拓扑磁电耦合。轴子耦合不依赖skyrmion螺旋性，而磁电多极耦合依赖螺旋性，环向分量按sinγ变化、对Néel型纹理为零。估计每个skyrmion每循环的Q-拓扑磁电能量约为10⁻²⁰ J（数特斯拉磁场下）。循环受热力学限制：恒θ循环净功为零，效率受轴子熵变限制。

Conclusion: Bi₂Se₃中受挫磁序与拓扑表面态通过同一控制参数在临界轨迹上协同转变，skyrmion电荷Q作为微观桥梁连接陈数/ℤ₂不变量、轴子角与拓扑磁电耦合；该耦合受螺旋性选择规则区分，且其能量循环存在由轴子熵变决定的热力学效率上限。

Abstract: A defining feature of quantum materials is that multiple orders govern low-energy physics and are not independent. We study Bi$_2$Se$_3$ by combining DFT with a renormalized correlated honeycomb model in which frustrated magnetic order and helical topological surface states are coupled via one control parameter. The surface states map to a honeycomb Hubbard model with frustrated $J_1$--$J_2$ exchange. Exact diagonalization up to 20 sites shows the Néel structure factor and valence-bond-solid order parameter cross at $J_2/J_1 \approx 0.22$, agreeing with QMC/DMRG, and a dynamical spin structure factor broadens near the crossover, as expected for a spinon continuum. Band inversion of the renormalized tight-binding model occurs on the same critical locus, producing jumps in the Chern number and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ invariant mirrored by the skyrmion charge $Q$. This gives a microscopic link: $Q$ selects the axion angle and thus the topological magnetoelectric coupling. We distinguish axion coupling, independent of skyrmion helicity, from the helicity-dependent magnetoelectric multipole coupling, whose toroidal component scales as $\sinγ$ and vanishes for Néel-type textures. We derive the $Q$-topological magnetoelectric energy and estimate $\sim 10^{-20}\,\mathrm{J}$ per skyrmion per cycle for few-tesla fields. The cycle is thermodynamically bounded: constant-$θ$ cycles extract no net work, and efficiency is limited by axionic entropy change.

</details>


### [2] [Thermal Phase Transition of Three Dimensional Orthogonal Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03804)
*Inho Song,Minsu Park,Hanbit Oh,Eun-Gook Moon*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究三维正交金属（orthogonal metal）的有限温度行为，证明其热相变在耦合无能隙费米面后依然存在，并计算了物理电子谱函数中可被角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）识别的特征，指出其可能解释 Sr₂VO₃FeAs 等层状材料的赝能隙行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正交金属是强纠缠金属态的典型范例，其输运与热力学性质类似普通费米液体，但单电子激发本质上不同。尽管其基态性质受关注，三维正交金属及其有限温度行为仍缺乏探索，尤其是有限温度相变的热力学与谱学特征尚不明确。

Method: 从一个费米子与自旋自由度耦合的简单模型出发，采用微扰方法证明纯玻色极限中已知的热相变在耦合无能隙费米面后依然存在；随后计算物理电子谱函数随温度的演化，并识别 ARPES 中的特征信号。

Result: 热相变在费米子耦合下依然保留；相干区与正交金属区的低能谱权重存在明显对比，且这一对比不依赖常规的对称性破缺序；表明有限温度正交金属物理可作为三维及准二维层状材料中赝能隙行为的可能机制。

Conclusion: 研究确立了有限温度正交金属物理作为三维及准二维（层间跳跃不可忽略）材料中赝能隙行为的可能机制，并可应用于近期 Sr₂VO₃FeAs 的实验观测。

Abstract: Orthogonal metals provide a paradigmatic example of strongly entangled metallic states whose transport and thermodynamic properties resemble those of conventional Fermi liquids, while their single-electron excitations remain fundamentally distinct. Despite considerable interest in their unusual ground-state properties, orthogonal metals in three spatial dimensions and their finite-temperature behavior remain relatively unexplored. Here, we investigate a three-dimensional orthogonal metal and characterize the thermodynamic and spectroscopic signatures associated with its finite-temperature phase transition. Starting from a simple model of fermions coupled to spin degrees of freedom, we perturbatively demonstrate that the thermal transition known to occur in the purely bosonic limit persists in the presence of coupling to a gapless Fermi surface. We then examine physical observables that change prominently between the coherent and orthogonal-metal regimes. In particular, we calculate the evolution of the physical-electron spectral function and identify characteristic signatures in angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy. We find a pronounced contrast in low-energy spectral weight between the coherent and orthogonal-metal regimes without requiring conventional symmetry-breaking order. Our results establish finite-temperature orthogonal-metal physics as a possible mechanism for pseudogap-like behavior in three-dimensional and quasi-two-dimensional layered materials with non-negligible interlayer hopping, which may be applied to the recent experiments in $\mathrm{Sr_2 VO_3 Fe As}$.

</details>


### [3] [Collective Modes of Fractional States Beyond Landau Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03805)
*Peleg Emanuel,Yuval Oreg*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文建立一个基于强耦合耦合线构造的框架，用于描述分数量子霍尔态等拓扑有序相的集体激发，并将它们解释为有效 sine-Gordon 理论的孤子与呼吸子。该框架能再现磁振子（magnetoroton）分支的关键特征，并推广到分数量子陈绝缘体，揭示量子几何对分数化相及其集体激发稳定性的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔态等拓扑有序、强关联相的集体激发是探测其性质与稳定性的有力工具，但离开朗道能级极限后，分数态激发的描述仍然极具挑战，需要一种系统且可解析的框架。

Method: 作者在耦合线构造的强耦合区域发展了一套描述集体激发的框架。在该框架下，集体激发表现为有效 sine-Gordon 理论的孤子（solitons）与呼吸子（breathers）。将该方法应用于分数量子霍尔效应的耦合线构造，并进一步推广到分数量子陈绝缘体。

Result: 该框架成功捕捉到磁振子（magnetoroton）分支的关键特征，并证明类磁振子的集体模式在远离朗道能级极限时仍然存在。研究发现，当 Fubini-Study 度规趋于理想（迹条件）极限时，磁振子能隙增大；同一趋势也利于与手征引力子（chiral graviton）相关的长波四极子模式的稳定性。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了分数化与量子几何之间独特的相互作用，并为研究分数拓扑物质中的集体激发提供了一个解析框架。

Abstract: Collective modes provide a powerful probe into the properties and stability of topologically ordered and strongly interacting phases of matter, such as fractional quantum Hall states. Yet, the description of fractional-state excitations away from the Landau-level limit remains a formidable challenge. We develop a framework for describing collective excitations in the strong-coupling regime of coupled-wire constructions and show that they emerge as solitons and breathers of effective sine-Gordon theories. This approach provides a systematic route to studying collective modes in a broad class of fractional states. Applied to a coupled-wire construction of the fractional quantum Hall effect, the framework captures key features of the magnetoroton branch. It naturally extends to fractional Chern insulators, demonstrating that magnetoroton-like collective modes persist well beyond the Landau-level limit. We find that the magnetoroton gap increases as the Fubini-Study metric approaches the ideal (trace-condition) limit, highlighting the important role of quantum geometry in the stability of fractionalized phases and their collective excitations. The same trend favors the stability of the long-wavelength quadrupolar mode associated with the chiral graviton. Together, these results identify a distinct interplay between fractionalization and quantum geometry, and provide an analytical framework for studies of collective excitations in fractional topological matter.

</details>


### [4] [Coexisting Kondo and Weyl-like transport in kagome heavy-fermion metal, Yb$_y$Co$_3$G$e_{3-x}$Sn$_x$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03822)
*M. P. Dubbelman,R. Meduri,T. M. Kyrk,Z. Cheng,P. Maderson,D. Pizzirani,A. Das,V. N. Vreede,X. Liu,N. Witt,S. Grytsiuk,C. M. Polley,M. Rösner,A. Grubišić-Čabo,S. Bhattacharyya,R. E. Baumbach,M. E. Kamminga,S. Wiedmann,J. Y. Chan,M. N. Ali*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文在kagome金属Yb_yCo3Ge_{3-x}Sn_x中通过磁输运、比热、磁化率、ARPES、XPS及DFT+U计算，同时观察到近藤(Kondo)散射与魏尔(Weyl)手征异常的证据，表明关联电子与拓扑导电态可在同一块体材料中不经外部费米能级调控而共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强电子关联(使电子变“重”)与拓扑(使电子表现得像无质量粒子)是调控电输运的两条互补路径，但二者在同一种材料中同时出现极为罕见。此前在Weyl-Kondo体系中虽预测由近藤重整化能带形成Weyl态，但尚未在同一材料的输运测量中同时观测到近藤态与Weyl态两种信号。

Method: 综合运用磁输运、比热、磁化率、角分辨光电子能谱(ARPES)、X射线光电子能谱(XPS)以及DFT+U计算，研究kagome金属Yb_yCo3Ge_{3-x}Sn_x；通过横向磁阻的单一离子近藤标度行为、纵向输运中额外负磁阻的B²依赖关系及其在32 T下不饱和等特征进行判别。

Result: 观测到依赖Yb的近藤散射并发展为晶格相干性，外推的Sommerfeld系数约为2 J mol_Yb⁻¹ K⁻²；横向磁阻符合单一离子近藤标度，而纵向输运额外呈现与B²成正比的负磁阻，且在32 T仍未饱和，与手征异常一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持近藤导电与类Weyl导电分别起源于不同的电子状态、且无需外部费米能级调控即可共存，为在块体材料中结合关联电子与拓扑功能提供了一条可能的途径。

Abstract: Strong electron correlations and topology offer complementary routes to controlling electrical conduction, as correlations can make electrons heavy (e.g. Kondo lattices) whereas topology can make them behave as massless particles (e.g. Dirac or Weyl systems), and having both in one material is rare. In Kondo lattice systems, Kondo hybridization between localized 4$f$ and conduction electrons strongly enhances the quasiparticle mass, where in Weyl-Kondo systems these Kondo-renormalized bands form Weyl states, and simultaneous signs of both Kondo and Weyl states in one material have not been observed in transport. Here we report signs of both in the kagome metal \ce{Yb_yCo3Ge_{3-x}Sn_x} by combining magnetotransport, heat capacity, magnetization, angle-resolved and X-ray photoemission spectroscopy, and DFT+U calculations to reveal Yb-dependent Kondo scattering developing into lattice coherence, with extrapolated Sommerfeld coefficient of approximately 2 ~Jmol$_{\mathrm{Yb}}^{-1}$K$^{-2}$. Transverse magnetoresistance (MR) follows single-ion Kondo scaling, whereas longitudinal transport contains an additional negative response proportional to B$^2$ and remains unsaturated up to 32 T, consistent with a chiral anomaly. These findings support a picture of Kondo and Weyl-like conduction arising from distinct electronic states without external Fermi-level tuning, suggesting a possible route to combining correlated and topological functionality in a bulk material.

</details>


### [5] [Repulsion-Driven Topological Superconductivity in Twisted MoTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03833)
*Tianle Wang,Tomohiro Soejima,Norman Y. Yao,Ashvin Vishwanath,Michael P. Zaletel,Taige Wang,Daniel E. Parker*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过对扭曲MoTe₂微观模型施加屏蔽库仑相互作用并进行无限圆柱DMRG计算，本文证明该体系可实现手性拓扑超导，其配对的f波手性特性带来手性马约拉纳边缘模与非阿贝尔涡旋核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从莫尔材料中由排斥相互作用出发，寻找一条通往手性拓扑超导（具有手性马约拉纳边缘模）的微观实现路径，以解释扭曲MoTe₂实验中观察到的超导现象。

Method: 构建扭曲MoTe₂微观模型并引入屏蔽库仑相互作用，在无限圆柱几何上使用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）计算窄陈能带中的Cooper对关联、超流刚度及各种竞争相的相图。

Result: 窄陈能带的Cooper对关联随圆柱周长增强，符合二维超导行为；超导配对为谷中心附近的手性f波，拓扑上产生手性马约拉纳边缘模和非阿贝尔涡旋核心。在零位移场下，超导跨越填充-0.78≲ν≲-0.72，接近实验范围，并以圆顶形式延伸到有限位移场。作者还预测了刚度随填充和可调位移场的演化及屏蔽长度的影响。邻近相包括分数陈绝缘体、拓扑电荷密度波、金属和电荷有序态，后者是实验中重入整数霍尔态的候选者。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一条从莫尔材料微观模型中的排斥相互作用到手性拓扑超导体（含手性马约拉纳边缘模）的可行路径，并解释了实验观测的填充和位移场依赖关系。

Abstract: We show that a microscopic model of twisted MoTe$_2$ with screened Coulomb interactions produces topological superconductivity. Using the density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) on infinite cylinders, we find that the narrow Chern band of twisted MoTe$_2$ has Cooper pair correlations that strengthen with cylinder circumference, consistent with two dimensional superconductivity. The superconductor has chiral $f$-wave pairing around the valley center, and its topology produces chiral Majorana edge modes and non-Abelian vortex cores. At zero displacement field, the superconductor spans fillings $-0.78\lesssimν\lesssim-0.72$, close to the experimental range, and extends in a dome to finite displacement field. We then predict how its stiffness evolves with the filling and the tunable displacement field. The interplay with screening length is also examined. Nearby, we find a fractional Chern insulator, a topological charge density wave, a metal, and a charge-ordered state. The last is a candidate for the reentrant integer Hall regime in experiments. These results establish a route from repulsive interactions in microscopic models of moiré materials to chiral topological uperconductors with chiral Majorana edge modes.

</details>


### [6] [Korringa factor in Dirac-nodal-line systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03994)
*Takahiko Sekine,Kanoda Kazushi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了狄拉克节线(DNL)体系中非关联的Korringa因子K*(α)，发现其具有本征温度依赖性，并在高温狄拉克值1.71与低温金属值1之间连续演化；朗道量子化会通过奇异态密度结构强烈增强K*(α)，产生两类磁振荡，为DNL材料NMR实验提供定量基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Korringa因子K(α)是核磁共振中衡量金属电子关联效应的常用量，传统上用非关联参考值K(α)=1来解释。但在狄拉克费米子体系中，非关联参考值取决于电子态密度(DOS)，与常规金属不同。因此需要厘清DNL体系中非关联Korringa因子的真实行为，以便区分态密度引起的贡献与真正的电子关联效应。

Method: 采用基于态密度(DOS)的方法，理论计算具有有限节线色散的狄拉克节线(DNL)体系中的非关联Korringa因子K*(α)；分析其温度依赖性与节线色散宽度的关系，并研究朗道量子化(磁场)作用下奇异态密度结构对K*(α)的增强及其磁振荡行为。

Result: K*(α)具有本征温度依赖性，从高温狄拉克费米子值1.71连续演化到低温金属值1，交叉温度正比于节线色散宽度(其被确定为控制DNL体系非关联Korringa因子的特征能量尺度)。朗道量子化产生的奇异DOS结构强烈增强K*(α)，即便无电子关联也产生显著峰。K*(α)还表现出两类磁振荡：来自能级内(intra-level)和能级间(inter-level)贡献；后者源于正、负量子数朗道子带奇异点在费米能级处的重叠，是DNL有限能量色散的独特结果。

Conclusion: 这些结果为解释DNL材料的NMR实验提供了定量参考，并有助于在态密度诱导贡献之外识别真正的关联效应。

Abstract: The Korringa factor $K(α)$ in the nuclear magnetic resonance studies is a widely used measure of electron-correlation effects in metals and is conventionally interpreted using the non-correlated value $K(α)$ = 1. In Dirac-fermion systems, however, the non-correlated reference value depends on the electronic density of states (DOS) and differs from that of conventional metals. Here, we theoretically investigate the non-correlated Korringa factor $K^*(α)$ in a Dirac nodal-line (DNL) system with finite nodal-line dispersion using a DOS-based approach. We show that $K^*(α)$ exhibits an intrinsic temperature dependence, continuously evolving from the Dirac-fermion value of 1.71 at high temperatures to the metallic value of 1 at low temperatures. The crossover temperature is found to be proportional to the nodal-line dispersion width, identified as the characteristic energy scale governing the non-correlated Korringa factor in DNL systems. We further demonstrate that Landau quantization generates singular DOS structures that strongly enhance $K^*(α)$, producing pronounced peaks even in the absence of electron correlations. In addition, $K^*(α)$ exhibits two distinct types of magnetic oscillations arising from intra-level and inter-level contributions. The latter originates from the overlap of singular points belonging to positive and negative quantum-number Landau subbands at the Fermi level and represents a unique consequence of the finite energy dispersion of a DNL. These results provide a quantitative reference for interpreting NMR experiments in DNL materials and for identifying genuine correlation effects beyond DOS-induced contributions.

</details>


### [7] [Resolving magnetic order and dynamics with polarized neutron scattering: a practical guide to longitudinal polarization analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04050)
*Sijie Xu,Pengcheng Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文综述了纵向极化分析（LPA）在非弹性中子散射中的应用，介绍其原理、数学形式化、散射几何与校正方法，并以量子材料（如交错磁体、非常规超导体、笼目磁体）为例展望未来发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数中子散射实验使用非极化中子，未能充分利用中子自旋所携带的信息；而 LPA 通过中子自旋的不同效应可分离磁性与非磁性散射贡献，但缺乏面向实践的系统性指南。

Method: 回顾 Moon、Riste 和 Koehler 在橡树岭国家实验室创立的 LPA 方法；引入将测量极化通道与磁性量相联系的数学形式化；讨论散射几何、晶体学投影以及对有限极化率的校正，并以实例说明 LPA 区分弱磁与非磁散射、约束小磁矩分量以及分辨磁激发的自旋空间各向异性。

Result: LPA 能够区分弱磁散射与非磁贡献、约束小磁矩分量，并解析磁激发的自旋空间各向异性，为磁性结构测定和自旋激发表征提供了有效工具。

Our aim is to provide a practical guide for neutron scatterers using LPA to determine magnetic structures and characterize spin excitations in quantum materials. Abstract: Strongly correlated matter without quasiparticles is central to many-body physics, quantum chaos, and quantum gravity, motivating a considerable effort toward its realization in tabletop experiments. Two challenges remain: laboratory platforms are open systems, so conformal behavior must survive dissipation, and it must be identified through measurable quantities. We address both challenges in the Yukawa-Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (YSYK) model, in which bosons and fermions interact as in, e.g., light-matter systems, but through random all-to-all couplings that drive the boson to criticality without fine tuning. Solving the model in real time using the Schwinger-Keldysh formalism, we treat closed and open systems within a unified framework and, in both cases, consider a thermal steady state to map their phase diagrams. In addition to the known conformal and impurity (or SYK$_2$) regimes, we identify novel high-temperature regimes in which the fermions are driven by an essentially bare boson and show that their dynamics is captured by a quadratic model with time-dependent random hopping. Across all regimes, the fermionic softening of the boson is bounded by a ``criticality budget'' determined only by the Yukawa coupling and the temperature. Coupling the boson with a thermal environment induces a mass shift that deforms the boundaries between different regimes and exhausts the criticality budget, thus imposing a bound on temperature and dissipation rate compatible with self-tuned criticality. We further introduce a set of markers built from the two-point functions that separate all identified regimes, are accessible in linear response, and require no fit of the conformal exponents. We complement them with the quantum Fisher information, deriving upper bounds with and without dissipation. These results may help characterize holographic quantum matter beyond the closed setting.

The zero-field excitations separate into a low-energy triakontadipole wave and a broad higher-energy continuum. Magnetic susceptibility measurements suggest an antiferromagnetic ground state with a high Neél temperature $T_{\rm N} > 350$~K\@. A large and positive magnetoresistance (MR) of approximately $50\%$ is observed at low temperatures, which follows a power-law field dependence with exponent $n = 1.50(8)$. Low-temperature electrical resistivity data reveal Kondo-like features. Heat capacity measurements show no signs of a phase transition below 300~K and further confirm a metallic ground state. Electronic structure calculations reveal a three-dimensional Fermi surface and suggest the presence of substantial electronic correlation effects.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势中的长程相互作用通常通过固定静电核或学习得到的构型无关核来处理，但这类核是否足够取决于潜在的屏蔽相互作用本质。作者旨在探明：当屏蔽介质随构型变化（不均匀屏蔽）时，构型无关核是否存在根本性局限，并验证构型依赖核的必要性。

Method: 以局部主干网络（MACE）的描述符构建潜在源场，采用傅里叶神经算子（FNO）学习这些源场跨模拟单元的相互作用。对比了三种方案：预设库仑核、线性FNO（学习构型无关核）与非线性FNO（学习构型依赖核）。在可调屏蔽的合成离子系统上检验了强屏蔽与不均匀屏蔽情形，并在液态水、锂镧锆氧（LLZO）及Au2/MgO(001)界面上做第一性原理基准测试。

Abstract: Long-range interactions in machine-learning interatomic potentials are commonly treated by coupling learned sources through either a prescribed electrostatic kernel or a learned but configuration-independent one. Whether such a kernel suffices depends on the underlying screened interaction. We address this question by using Fourier neural operators (FNOs) to learn how latent source fields, built from the descriptors of a local backbone (here MACE), interact across the simulation cell. For a synthetic ionic system with tunable screening, a prescribed Coulomb kernel underestimates the long-wavelength charge fluctuations by 43% under strong screening, whereas the FNO reproduces them within 3% and recovers 95-99% of the variance of the residual left by the local backbone. A linear FNO, which learns a configuration-independent kernel, performs equally well in the homogeneous case. Under inhomogeneous screening, rearranging the remote medium modulates the coupling between distant ions, a dependence that no configuration-independent kernel can represent. Among the models tested, only the nonlinear FNO responds to this modulation, and its force errors are 27-41% lower than those of configuration-independent models. In first-principles benchmarks of liquid water, lithium lanthanum zirconate, and Au2/MgO(001), MACE-FNO is comparable to a prescribed-kernel model for the bulk systems and more accurate for the interface. It further captures the reversal of the Au2 wetting preference induced by remote Al dopants, with deviations below 5 meV per cell from density functional theory. These results indicate that a configuration-dependent kernel, as learned by FNOs, becomes necessary once the screening medium varies, even though the energy remains quadratic in the charges for a fixed medium.

</details>

A low-doped Ge layer on a conductive p-type Si substrate redirects anodization current toward the misfit-dislocation-rich interface. Numerical modelling including charge-transfer kinetics shows that this confinement arises from hole-limited charge transfer and, together with electrochemical calibration, defines a process window that combines interfacial current confinement with controlled Si porosification. After soft annealing, geometric phase analysis and plan-view TEM show a modified strain-relaxation pathway and strongly suppressed threading-dislocation emergence. Raman spectroscopy indicates a reduction in residual tensile strain from approximately 0.10 % to 0.05 %, whereas spreading resistance profiling confirms preservation of the Ge carrier profile. Abstract: The coherent elastic wave in untextured polycrystals, central to ultrasonic and seismic characterization, was recently observed to have two modes per polarization at short wavelengths. However, their physical origin and amplitude partition have remained open. We address both by theory, grain-scale numerical simulations and a grain velocity estimate. The first-order smoothing approximation, simplified by the far-field approximation, predicts three waves, which are the classical effective wave and one for each type of scattered wave (longitudinal and transverse). At long wavelengths, only the effective wave is weakly attenuated. At short wavelengths, one of the other two waves joins it with comparable attenuation, whereas the third stays far more attenuated. A new residue method, which needs no fitting, gives the same pattern for the amplitude partition, with the two waves sharing the coherent field and the third carrying little. The simulations reveal exactly these two modes, for transverse waves in iron and longitudinal waves in strongly anisotropic SrMgSn, and never the third. The wavefields reveal the mechanism, in which the incident wave is extinguished within one grain correlation length and the coherent field is rebuilt by forward multiple scattering. Strikingly, a grain velocity estimate from single-crystal constants provides independent evidence, tracing the two modes to plane waves of the individual grains and closely reproducing their velocities. Observability of multiple waves is thus set by attenuation and amplitude partition together, and short-wavelength measurements in random media must allow for more than one coherent mode.

Abstract: Minimal model and its linear stability analysis (LSA) for Plateau-Rayleigh (PR) instability of an intermixed, solid binary alloy nanowire is presented. Model accounts only for the surface diffusions of the alloy components without anisotropies of a surface energies, and for a classical thermodynamic surface segregation. Model allows to assess a deviation of a longwave instability cutoff wavelength $λ_c$ from the classical value $2πr_0$ (a wire circumference) as a function of a surface composition deviation from its equilibrium value, a wire radius, and a bulk concentration of a minority component. Results of LSA are presented for AuNi, PtNi, and CoNi nanowires, where an important distinction is that a surface segregation curve for CoNi is qualitatively different from the one for AuNi and PtNi. For each nanowire there is a sizable region of a parameter space where $λ_c<2πr_0$ and correspondingly, a fastest growing perturbation wavelength $λ_{max}$ is also less than a wire circumference. This is in stark contrast to PR instability of a single-component nanowire transporting mass by surface diffusion - in this case $λ_c$ and $λ_{max}$ are larger than a wire circumference. Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) spin-ice compounds, predominantly based on the pyrochlore or spinel structure, have been attracting considerable interest in the last two decades. A two-dimensional (2D) analogue is the spin-ice consisting of kagome planes. So far, only Ising spin ice has been discovered in any material, and only one 2D material, the kagome compound HoAgGe, has been identified as a spin ice. In this Letter, we show that another rare-earth twisted Kagome magnetic compound, DyAgGe, is in fact a Potts spin ice (or color ice), where at each site not two but four spin directions are allowed. We argue that an 8-state spin ice may also exist in this class of systems. We present a first-principles calculation of magnetic interactions in DyAgGe, and confirm the 4-state Potts structure of this material by matching our model predictions with the available experimental data.

Abstract: Tantalum pentoxide (Ta2O5) thin films deposited by ion beam sputtering (IBS) are essential components in high-power laser systems, where post-deposition annealing is routinely used to optimize film quality. Argon bubbles inherently incorporated during IBS serve as structural markers: their size correlates positively with the surface roughness from the as-deposited to 700 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ but decouples at 800 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$, revealing a transition from matrix-relaxation-dominated to surface-diffusion-dominated behavior. Transmittance spectra show a red shift at 400 and 600 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ which can be explained by physical thickness increase. Weak absorption decreases from ~6 ppm in as-deposited films to ~2 ppm at 400-600 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ due to annihilation of oxygen vacancies, rises markedly at 700 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ due to grain-boundary defects and then drops again at 800 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ as grain boundaries relax. We identify 400 $^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ as the optimal annealing window for IBS-deposited Ta2O5 single layers, with the lowest surface roughness and effectively reducing weak absorption while preserving the amorphous phase.

Abstract: MgO-SiO$_2$ binders are promising low-CO$_2$ alternatives to Portland cement that can permanently store CO$_2$ by incorporating magnesium carbonate phases. Real space phase quantification reveals that magnesium-silicate-hydrate (M-S-H) is detected in the first scan (~45 min) of every paste, before formation of any brucite or HCB. The PDF results also show that in the neat MgO-SiO$_2$ paste, half of the M-S-H present at 42 days has formed by 24 h, while no M-S-H is detected by XRD at any age due to the intrinsic disorder present in this phase. Both carbonate sources (HY and DHY) are found to increase the extent of MgO dissolution, where no unreacted MgO is detected at 24 h in contrast with >7 days for the neat paste. However, DHY is seen to slow the initial stages of MgO dissolution (prior to ~6 h) compared with the neat paste, a period during which significant DHY rehydration is seen to occur without any HCB formation. Abstract: Ionic conduction in solid-state electrolytes is conventionally described by mobile cations migrating through a static host lattice. Yet the fastest conductors are often the most disordered, and how disorder enhances transport remains unresolved at the mesoscale, inaccessible to ensemble-averaged probes. We use Bragg X-ray photon correlation spectroscopy to measure nanoscale structural dynamics of a single ball-milled Na$_3$PS$_4$ particle. We find that the cubic phase behaves as a strain glass: a frustrated configuration of three degenerate tetragonal variants with short-range order. Its domain-wall motion is stress-driven, crossing over irreversibly to thermally activated dynamics on annealing as residual milling stress is depleted. Rearrangements follow scale-free avalanche statistics, matching ferroelastic martensites but without external driving. The tetragonal polymorph shows none of these features and relaxes fifty times more slowly. These dynamics occur on timescales comparable to estimated ionic diffusion across the particle, indicating the host structure cannot be assumed static on transport-relevant timescales.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TENG依靠低频机械能转换与自供能传感，其性能受材料、表面、力学、电荷与电路多因素耦合影响；现有研究对此缺乏跨尺度、面向预测性设计的系统梳理与验证策略。

Method: 按长度尺度综述材料性质、表面形貌、接触力学、电荷产生与外部电路的相互作用；讨论聚合物、无机、复合及天然材料；评估分子动力学与第一性原理、粗糙面接触力学、器件级静电学与等效电路等建模方法的输入需求、预测量与实验验证；并依据宏观、毫米、微观器件尺寸组织应用需求。

Result: 识别出预设表面电荷密度、理想化几何与名义接触面积假设在真实加载条件下会限制预测能力；确定真实接触面积演化及接触依赖的电荷产生与衰减是连接界面行为与电学输出的关键；器件尺寸尺度决定材料、架构与集成的不同要求。

Conclusion: 提出分阶段验证策略——依次验证接触力学、静电学与电路响应，并在校准条件之外进行独立测试，以指导多尺度模型开发与可靠TENG系统构建。

Abstract: Triboelectric nanogenerators (TENGs) convert low-frequency mechanical energy into electricity and enable self-powered sensing. Their performance depends on interactions among material properties, surface morphology, contact mechanics, charge generation and external circuits. This review examines these interactions across length scales to assess how material and interface descriptions support predictive device design. Polymeric, inorganic, composite and natural materials are discussed in relation to charge generation and retention, mechanical compliance, durability and application requirements. Modelling approaches span molecular dynamics and first-principles calculations, rough-surface contact mechanics, device-scale electrostatics and equivalent circuits. Their capabilities are assessed through the inputs they require, the quantities they predict and the experimental evidence used for validation. Particular attention is given to prescribed surface charge density, idealised geometries and nominal contact-area assumptions, which constrain predictions under realistic loading conditions. Beyond that, it can be used to study the response of phonon systems in materials to arbitrary heat sources.

Abstract: Simulating microstructure evolution requires tracking many moving grain and phase boundaries coupled to thermodynamic and mechanical fields. Abstract: The real mechanism of ferromagnetism in III-V diluted magnetic semiconductors such as Ga$_{1-x}$Mn$_x$As is still debated. The experimental results point out the realization of appreciable ferromagnetism at filling of the impurity band close to one-half that can be expressed in terms of compensation degree. Based on our recent results on the existence of two competing mechanisms of pair indirect exchange (with ferro- and antiferro alignment of Mn $d$ shells), a theory of magnetic ordering in (Ga,Mn)As is presented. The molecular-field-like equation is derived in which the effective exchange constant contains all necessary information about material, including the Mn content, the compensation degree, the crystal coordination, and the parameters of two competing pair exchange interactions. The Curie temperature depends crucially on the compensation degree. Depending on Mn content and compensation degree, three possible magnetic phases (paramagnetic, ferromagnetic, and spin-glass-like) are predicted. However, the spin-glass-like phase in (Ga,Mn)As is apparently not realized due to the additional inevitable compensation caused by As antisites. The phase transition into the ferromagnetic state occurs not only with Mn content variation but through the change of the compensation degree as well. The new mechanism of photoferromagnetic effect is predicted and discussed in the context of possible clarification of the magnetic ordering mechanism. It is assumed that the main obstacle to a possible increase in the Curie temperature is the increasing contribution of the antiferromagnetic superexchange between the nearest-neighbor Mn's, rather than compensatory (electric or magnetic) effects from Mn interstitials, as was previously stated. The comparison of our predictions with available experimental data is presented.

Abstract: Defect states directly influence not only functional properties, but also oxynitride formation during ammonolysis. Along with structural stability and charge balance, these defects can cause non-uniform nitrogen incorporation. To address this, the recently proposed Fermi Level Engineering concept provides a powerful alternative of rationalising oxynitride synthesis. By using the electron chemical potential, i.e., the Fermi level, as a unique descriptor, this approach can predict, understand, and manipulate defect formation enthalpies and charge transition levels, subsequently allowing to incorporate defect-property correlations to the concept. This work combines theoretical modelling and experimental characterisation of barium titanium oxynitrides to explain an intrinsic, self-limiting nitridation process. Progressive nitrogen incorporation causes a Fermi level downward shift, leaving further nitrogen substitution on oxygen sites energetically unfavourable, up to 0.03 N per unit formula. We demonstrate that A-site cation substitution in Eu$_{1-x}$Ba$_x$TiO$_3$- and Eu$_{1-x}$Ca$_x$TiO$_3$-derived oxynitrides can be applied to control compensation of the additional negative charge from N$^{3-}$ and keep the Fermi level high. The Eu$^{2+/3+}$ redox couple enables substitution of oxygen by nitrogen through pinning of the Fermi level at the europium charge transition level, at $\sim$ 2.9 eV above the VBM. This allows for higher nitrogen uptake of up to 0.65 per formula unit (taken from hot gas extraction) through a more controlled process. Further incorporation is then limited by an intra-Ti(O,N)$_6$ octahedra distortion due to internal bond-angle variance. These findings demonstrate that Fermi Level Engineering provides a pathway to predict and overcome intrinsic synthetic and thermodynamic limitations in oxynitride synthesis.

Abstract: Three metastable $β$ titanium alloys with different molybdenum contents, Ti-12Mo, Ti-15Mo and Ti-18Mo (wt.%), were investigated to determine the influence of $β$-phase stability on phase transformations during continuous heating. Electrical resistance measurement, dilatometry and differential scanning calorimetry were performed during linear heating at 5 °C/min up to 750 °C. All alloys exhibited a sequence involving partial dissolution of athermal $ω$ phase, stabilization and growth of isothermal $ω$ phase, subsequent $ω$ dissolution and precipitation of $α$ phase. The excellent agreement between electrical resistance measurements, DSC, dilatometry and electron microscopy enabled a consistent description of phase evolution during heating and demonstrated the benefits of combining complementary experimental techniques for investigation of complex transformation pathways in metastable $β$ titanium alloys.

Abstract: Polypyrrole NH$_3$ chemiresistors in nanotube (NT) and planar film (PF) morphologies are compared on response magnitude, limit of detection (LOD), sensitivity, and response/recovery kinetics, including the performance of the same sensors tracked over 60 days of ambient storage and repeated NH$_{3}$ exposure. In the case of fresh sensors, the time constants for the fast process ($τ_1$) is shorter in the NT than in the PF at every concentration. NT is 38 \% faster on average than PF. The slow process ($τ_2$) overlaps between two morphologies and shows no concentration dependence. The two time constants differentiate the two morphologies through aging: NT $τ_1$ and $τ_2$ decrease substantially and monotonically over 60 days, while the time constants of PF show no consistent trend. All measured sensing parameters, LOD, sensor response and sensitivity improve or at least remain stable with aging in the case of NT, while all parameters degrade in the PF case. Abstract: Simulating quantum many-body systems classically encounters exponential bottlenecks, which quantum devices circumvent by natively encoding wavefunctions. In the Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) era, however, hardware noise constrains simulation accuracy. As a stepping stone toward strongly correlated materials, we compute the band structure of 2D monolayer graphene and 1D nanoribbons (zigzag and armchair) using a single variational workflow in the $p_z$ tight-binding approximation. Non-interacting ground states also provide essential initial reference states for subspace and warm-started variational methods to solve interacting spectra. By mapping the Bloch Hamiltonian to Pauli operators, we extract the ground band via the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) and excited subbands via Variational Quantum Deflation (VQD), validating our approach from ideal simulations to execution on physical IBM Quantum processors. In ideal simulations, the workflow reproduces exact diagonalization results with high precision, whereas hardware noise introduces energy deviations of a few hundredths of the hopping parameter. While qubit counts scale logarithmically with ribbon width, fixed-depth ansatz accuracy degrades with Hilbert space dimension. Crucially, deepening the ansatz improves ideal simulation accuracy by over three orders of magnitude but worsens energy estimates under noise, proving that practical circuit depth is bounded by hardware noise rather than expressivity. Finally, we complement energy evaluations with a wavefunction-level diagnostic measuring subspace containment. This diagnostic captures degenerate manifolds and exposes optimization failure modes disguised by energy agreement alone. Operating directly on NISQ hardware, this validated workflow establishes a controlled baseline for studying topological and strongly correlated phases in realistic materials.

Abstract: Ferrons are elementary excitations of ferroelectric order that can propagate and transport polarization when excited with light. Here, we show theoretically that ferron polaritons exhibit a Hall effect: an applied magnetic field generates polarization transport perpendicular to the propagation direction of a ferron polariton excited by an incident terahertz pulse. We demonstrate this effect for the example of ferroelectric SnTe using first-principles calculations and a microscopic theory of the phonon-polariton current. The direction and magnitude of the transverse polarization transport can be controlled by the polarization of the terahertz pulse and the applied magnetic field. Our results establish a route toward light-controlled directional transport of electric polarization in ferroelectrics.

### [44] [Bond-valence theory is a general electrostatic valence rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05339)
*M. L. Whittaker*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用第一性原理电子密度中的原子盆面通量重新定义键价，证明在原子盆面上键价和规则由高斯定律严格成立，并通过3680种化合物验证键价定律，揭示软度B是局部位置性质而非可转移常数，最终将键价理论归结为带距离指数的Pauling规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统键价理论以和规则为公理，将阳离子电荷在配位键上等分（Pauling静电价规则）或用指数形式 s=exp[(R0-d)/B] 描述，但B和R0被视为阴-阳离子对的普适可转移常数。本文意在从第一性原理电子密度出发，为键价与和规则提供坚实的物理基础，并检验参数的可转移性。

Method: 作者在第一性原理计算的电子密度中划分原子盆（atomic basins），每个键临界点对应一个盆面；将穿过该面的电通量定义为与键关联的电荷（即键价）。由高斯定律，离开阳离子的通量之和必等于其盆电荷，因此和规则在盆面上成为定理而非公理。随后在3680种氧化物、卤化物、硫族化物和磷族化物中逐位置拟合通量与键长关系，检验键价定律、提取R0与B，并测试(R0,B)在不同化合物族间的可转移性；同时用和规则消去B，推导出与Pauling规则等价的表达式。

Result: 通量逐位置服从键价定律，中位偏差为10%。软度B变化近一个数量级，从较硬的硫化物到较软的砷化物，均由原子盆面上的静电通量决定；R0则由键长及其差值确定。大多数氧化物、氟化物、硫化物和氮化物位置处，阴离子价与成键阳离子价相差在5%以内。(R0,B)在106个族中有96个跨化合物转移失败，表明软度是由盆电荷决定的局部位置性质，而非给定阳-阴离子对的可转移常数。通过和规则消去B后得到 s=(z/n)^[(R0-d)/(R0-dB)]，即在软度加权平均键长dB处的距离指数形式。

Conclusion: 键价理论本质上是带距离指数的Pauling静电价规则，电子密度提供的原子盆面正是二者等价成立的表面。软度B是局部位置属性，由原子盆电荷决定，不可作为阴-阳离子对的普适常数跨化合物转移。

Abstract: Pauling's electrostatic valence rule assigns to each bond of a cation of charge $z$ with $n$ anion neighbors the strength $z/n$. Bond-valence theory replaces the equal split of valence among bonds by $s=\exp[(R_0-d)/B]$ in the bond length $d$ and takes the sum rule, that the valences at each atom add to its formal charge, as its first axiom. Here the bond valence is defined in the electron density. Atomic basins of a first-principles density are bounded by faces, one per bond critical point, and the electric flux of that density through a face is a charge associated with the bond. The fluxes leaving a cation sum to its basin charge by Gauss's law, so on this surface the sum rule is a theorem. Across 3680 oxides, halides, chalcogenides and pnictides the flux follows the bond-valence law site by site, with a median deviation of ten percent. The softness $B$ varies nearly an order of magnitude from stiff sulfides to softer arsenides. In all cases it is set by the electrostatic flux at atomic basin faces. $R_0$ then follows from the bond lengths and their differences. Anion valences equal those of bonded cations to within 5\% for most of the oxide, fluoride, sulfide and nitride sites. Transferability of $(R_0,B)$ between compounds fails in 96 of 106 families, suggesting that the softness is a local site property defined by the basin charge rather than a transferable constant of a given cation--anion pair. Eliminating $B$ by the sum rule gives $s=(z/n)^{(R_0-d)/(R_0-d_B)}$, Pauling's strength raised to a distance exponent at the softness-weighted mean bond length $d_B$. Thus, bond-valence theory is Pauling's rule with a distance exponent, and the electron density supplies the surface on which the two are equivalent.

</details>

Conclusion: 当复杂谱特征阻碍相干峰的无歧义识别时，谱学的统计分析方法是一种约束超导能隙结构的强大手段。

Abstract: Monolayer FeSe grown on SrTiO$_3$ (001) exemplifies interface-enhanced superconductivity, exhibiting a transition temperature nearly ten times that of bulk FeSe. Yet, its tunneling spectra lack clearly identifiable superconducting coherence peaks, preventing an unambiguous determination of the gap magnitude. Here, we use ultralow-temperature scanning tunneling spectroscopy as a function of temperature, tip-sample separation, and lateral position to disentangle tunneling contributions by statistical analysis. Decay rates extracted by regression of tip-height-dependent spectroscopy reveal three energy regimes: Andreev-reflection-dominated tunneling below 6 meV, mixed Andreev and quasiparticle tunneling between 6 and 13 meV, and predominantly quasiparticle tunneling above 13 meV. Spatially resolved spectroscopy and correlation analysis further reveal that the tunneling conductance is predominantly localized near defects below 6 meV but becomes spatially extended at higher energies. Together, these results constrain the superconducting gap to lie between 6 and 13 meV. Our findings establish statistical analysis of spectroscopy as a powerful approach to constraining superconducting gap structures when complex spectral features prevent the unambiguous identification of coherence peaks.

Abstract: The predominantly $d_{x^2-y^2}$-wave pairing symmetry of cuprate superconductors is well established, yet its microscopic manifestation in local Cooper-pair tunneling remains less explored. Here, we investigate Bi$_{1.2}$Pb$_{0.8}$Sr$_2$CaCu$_2$O$_{8+δ}$, using Josephson scanning tunneling spectroscopy with a conventional $s$-wave superconducting Pb tip. We observe a zero-bias conductance enhancement whose approximately quadratic dependence on junction conductance is consistent with phase-diffusive Josephson tunneling. Atomic-resolution conductance maps and site-resolved spectra reveal a pronounced intra-unit-cell modulation, with enhanced Josephson response near oxygen sites and strong suppression near copper sites and along diagonal directions. Microscopic calculations incorporating the bilayer electronic structure and realistic Wannier-based tunneling matrix elements reproduce the observed spatial pattern through interference between pairing contributions of opposite signs. Our results reveal how the sign-changing $d$-wave superconducting order parameter manifests itself in atomic-scale Josephson tunneling, providing complementary evidence for unconventional pairing symmetry in cuprates.

Abstract: Nonreciprocal charge transport, where current flows preferentially in one direction, has become a key topic across quantum materials. This review covers some recent progress in bulk crystals, artificial heterostructures, van der Waals systems, high-temperature superconductors, and magnetic materials. The review discusses both intrinsic origins, such as band-structure effects, and extrinsic ones, including vortex motion, device geometry, and heating artifacts. A central focus is the superconducting diode effect due to its application potential, which shows different critical currents for opposite directions. The review also examines how symmetry breaking, spin-orbit coupling, and magnetic order contribute to the observed responses. However, there remain challenges when many exciting findings lack a unified picture, and the field continues to debate the relative importance of various mechanisms. The review highlights key open questions and suggests future directions, including better experimental standards and device integration. This review aims to give a balanced view of what is known, what is uncertain, and where the field might go next.

</details>

Abstract: In this work, we have presented S-I-S Josephson junctions with $\rmα$-Ta electrodes and a ${\rm TaO}_x$ barrier. However, we note that the choice of seed layer can have subtle implications on the electrical tunneling behavior of the junctions. We used room-temperature probing of variable junction areas, combined with Ambegaokar-Baratoff physics, to characterize the wafer scale critical current densities. For 0.4 $μ$m$^2$ junctions, we found junction spreads as low as 2.0% across the 20 x 20 mm chip relevant for device performance. The entire process was carried out using standard industry foundry processing using 150 mm wafer tooling, which is an important step towards proving that Ta-based junctions can be implemented at scale. We have successfully fabricated and tested junctions with $\rmα$-Ta electrodes and a ${\rm TaO}_x$ insulating tunnel barrier, demonstrating that this is a material platform which merits further investigation and studies for potential application in classical and quantum superconducting technologies.

TL;DR: 本文研究一种由等质量与交替非线性弹簧构成的SSH力学模型，在连续极限下得到描述声学场与光学带隙场的非线性双曲Boussinesq型方程组。作者发现其拉格朗日量中声学与光学自由度通过类“合成自旋-轨道耦合”的结构耦合，并涌现非阿贝尔Berry联络。利用变分法得到孤立波解，光学场满足无阻尼无驱动的三次-五次Duffing方程；在布里渊区边缘系统化为Soler型非线性Dirac场论，实现相对论性孤子的力学类比。通过分离快速空间振荡得到Jackiw-Rebbi型哈密顿量，畴壁处存在拓扑零模，与魔角石墨烯中的平带、超导和电荷分数化现象相似。最后讨论了系统趋向完全二聚化极限时的力学刚度转变及零模变为软模（floppy mode）的行为。

Result: 结果表明：(1) 连续极限下的力学SSH模型可映射为带类合成自旋-轨道耦合与非阿贝尔Berry联络的双场理论；(2) 得到孤立波解，其光学场满足三次-五次Duffing方程；(3) 在布里渊区边缘系统等价于Soler型非线性Dirac场论，给出相对论性相互作用孤子的直接力学实现；(4) 有效Jackiw-Rebbi哈密顿量在畴壁处产生拓扑零模，这些零模类似于魔角石墨烯中与超导和电荷分数化相关的电子平带；(5) 系统演化到完全二聚化极限时零模变为软模，并伴随力学刚度转变。

Conclusion: 论文建立了一个将拓扑力学、非线性波物理与相对论性场论相统一的力学平台。非线性SSH链在连续极限下同时显示出合成自旋-轨道耦合、非阿贝尔Berry联络、Soler型Dirac孤子以及Jackiw-Rebbi拓扑零模等丰富现象，实现了凝聚态与高能物理概念的经典力学类比，并揭示出拓扑零模向力学软模（floppy mode）过渡的刚度转变机制，为在力学体系中研究拓扑平带、电荷分数化及相关量子现象提供了新途径。

Abstract: A mechanical version of the SSH model, made of equal masses joined by two alternating non-linear springs is studied in the continuum limit. Such a non-linear dimerized mechanical chain generates a nonlinear, dispersive, two-field hyperbolic system of Boussinesq type of differential equations, describing coupled acoustic and gapped optical fields. The corresponding Lagrangian density is found, revealing that the acoustic and optical degrees of freedom are coupled through a structure that is formally analogous to a synthetic spin-orbit coupling, where the spatial motion is locked to the internal sublattice degree of freedom. An emergent non-Abelian Berry connection term also appears. Some particular solitary-wave solutions are found using variational theory, as the effective optical field obeys an undamped and unforced cubic-quintic Duffing equation. Near the Brillouin-zone edge, the system becomes a nonlinear Dirac field theory of the Soler type, providing a direct mechanical realization of interacting relativistic solitons. By factoring out fast spatial oscillations, an effective Jackiw-Rebbi Hamiltonian is obtained, with topological zero modes at domain walls. These modes are akin to the electronic flat bands in moire graphene systems associated with superconductivity and charge fractionalization. Finally, we discuss the mechanical rigidity transition and its effects on the fields as the system evolves toward the fully dimerized limit, in which the zero modes are floppy modes.

Conclusion: 电致关联氧化物相变是实现动态自旋波操控的低功耗策略，为可重构磁子器件提供了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Electrically reconfigurable spin-wave transport is central to realizing energy-efficient magnonic information technologies, yet existing current-driven approaches require current densities that undermine the fundamental dissipation advantage of spin-wave-based signal processing. Here, we demonstrate low-power electrical control of spin-wave propagation in a hybrid magnonic device comprising a low-damping yttrium iron garnet (YIG) film coupled to an adjacent vanadium dioxide ($\small\text{VO}_{2}$) stripe. Passing a small current through $\small\text{VO}_{2}$ triggers its insulator-to-metal transition (IMT), producing a sharp, reversible resistivity reduction exceeding three orders of magnitude. This abrupt phase transition locally modifies the spin-wave dispersion in YIG, reducing the group velocity and suppressing transmission by up to 40 dB near the ferromagnetic resonance frequency. Pulsed current excitation enables reconfiguration of spin-wave transport on a 10 ms timescale. Temperature-dependent measurements, corroborated by thermal modeling, decouple the respective contributions of current-induced heating of YIG and the IMT in $\small\text{VO}_{2}$, establishing the latter as the dominant control mechanism. Our results demonstrate that electrically induced phase transitions in correlated oxides constitute an energy-efficient strategy for dynamic spin-wave manipulation, providing a scalable route toward reconfigurable magnonic devices.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二十顶点模型的未解区域，特别是此前有人提出存在有序化的相图区域；手性冰是该区域的极端点，作者希望检验该区域内部及边界上的临界性质，并判别这两类模型是否属于已知可精确求解的族。

Result: 两个模型分别得到剩余熵；向上箭头数守恒，且改变它的代价表明二者均为临界，长距离行为对应自由无质量场。手性冰是此前提议有序化区域的极端点，但沿穿过该区域的两条路径上处处表现出相同的临界行为，在提议的边界处没有任何特征。沿 Baxter 可解曲线以闭合形式得到相应临界指数。

Abstract: We study two restrictions of Baxter's symmetric twenty-vertex model on the triangular lattice, in which the three classes of vertices allowed by the ice rule carry the weights $a$, $b$ and $c$: chiral ice, in which only the twelve chiral vertices are allowed, and the model that excludes only the two alternating vertices. We show that neither model belongs to any of the known exactly solvable families of the twenty-vertex model, and we characterise chiral ice as the ice model in which every vertex has exactly one through line, a property that imposes a parity constraint along every closed lattice line. We compute the residual entropies of both models with transfer matrices on cylinders of up to twelve vertices in circumference, extrapolated with the finite-size correction predicted by conformal invariance, which we adapt to the triangular geometry and verify at two exactly solved points. The number of upward arrows crossing the cylinder is conserved, and the cost of changing it shows that both models are critical, with the long-distance behaviour of a free massless field. Chiral ice is the extreme point of a region of the phase diagram in which ordering had been suggested; along two paths through that region we find the same critical behaviour at every point, with no feature at the proposed boundary of the region. Along Baxter's exactly solved curve we obtain the corresponding exponents in closed form.

### [52] [The Inverse Mean First-Passage Time Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03831)

TL;DR: 该文研究一维势场中过阻尼粒子的平均首达时间反问题：给定τ(x)随起始距离的函数，寻找能实现它的势。作者推导了τ(x)可被某个势实现的条件，并证明α≤2的幂律τ(x)~x^α可由单调势实现，而任何超二次增长的τ(x)都需要远离目标位置的势阱；α=2分离了滑动主导与陷阱主导的运动机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教科书通常处理正问题：给定势求平均首达时间τ(x)。本文翻转这一方向，研究反问题——已知τ(x)函数形式，反求是否存在（以及何种）势能实现它，以揭示首达时间增长与势场结构之间的内在约束。

Method: 针对一维势场中过阻尼粒子，从首达时间与势之间的基本关系出发，推导τ(x)可实现的必要条件与充分条件，并以幂律τ(x)~x^α为例分析不同幂次对应的势场特征。

Result: 给出τ(x)可实现所满足的条件；证明幂律τ(x)~x^α在α≤2时可由单调势实现；任何具有超二次增长（α>2）的可实现τ(x)都要求目标位置之外任意远处存在势阱；α=2为滑动（sliding）主导与陷阱（trapping）主导运动机制的分界。

Conclusion: 首达时间的渐近增长形式严格限制了势场的几何结构：二次增长是单调滑动势所能支持的最大幂次，超二次增长必然要求系统出现远离目标的势阱（陷阱机制），从而在滑动与陷阱两类输运机制之间划出明确界限。

Abstract: We consider the mean first-passage time $τ(x)$ for an overdamped particle in a one-dimensional potential landscape, and address the problem inverse to the textbook one: Given $τ(x)$ as a function of starting distance, we look for a potential that realizes it. We derive the conditions on $τ(x)$ under which such a potential exists. We show that while the power-law dependences $τ(x)\sim x^α$ with $α\leq 2$ may be realized by monotonic potentials, any realizable $τ(x)$ with superquadratic growth requires potential wells arbitrarily far from the target; $α= 2$ separates sliding from trapping-dominated motion regimes.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛模拟与有限尺寸标度分析，研究负规范耦合的三维紧致格点Abelian-Higgs模型，发现其相图远比非负耦合情形丰富：双分量标量场下O(3)转变线延伸至负耦合区并变为一级相变，单分量也出现类似一级线，且在较大物质耦合处两类模型各出现两条分别对应三维Ising与XY普适类的连续转变线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负规范耦合会偏好受挫（frustrated）的plaquette构型，与物质铁磁型相互作用抑制受挫的倾向发生竞争，而这一竞争机制对相图与相变性质的影响此前尚不清楚，因此有必要系统考察负耦合区以揭示其丰富的相结构。

Method: 对单分量与双分量单位电荷标量场的三维紧致Abelian-Higgs模型进行蒙特卡洛模拟，结合有限尺寸标度（FSS）分析；利用能量累积量（energy-cumulant）标度确定连续转变的临界指数，并追踪K趋于负无穷（完全受挫极限）下的相变行为。

Result: 发现负耦合区相结构显著更丰富：双分量模型中规范不变的二次型序对应的O(3)转变线延伸进入负耦合区，并随K减小转变为一级相变线；单分量模型也出现类似一级相变线。此外，两类模型在足够大的物质耦合下均展现出两条额外的连续转变线，其能量累积量标度分别与三维Ising及XY临界指数一致。

Conclusion: 这些连续转变线暗示分别处于Z2与XY规范普适类的拓扑转变，表明物质序与动力学规范受挫之间的竞争催生了额外的相，但这些相的物理刻画仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: We investigate the phase diagram and nature of the phase transitions of three-dimensional compact lattice Abelian Higgs models with negative gauge coupling. These models describe multicomponent complex scalar fields coupled to a compact U(1) gauge field. Negative gauge coupling favors frustrated plaquette configurations, competing with the tendency of the matter ferromagnetic-like interaction to suppress frustration. Monte Carlo simulations and finite-size scaling analyses of models with one- and two-component scalar fields of unit charge reveal a substantially richer phase structure than at nonnegative gauge coupling. For two components, the O(3) transition line associated with gauge-invariant bilinear ordering extends into the negative-coupling region, and turns into a first-order transition line with decreasing $K$, toward the fully-frustrated $K\to -\infty$ limit. An analogous first-order transition line is also found for the one-component scalar matter with negative gauge coupling. Both one- and two-component models exhibit two additional continuous transition lines at sufficiently large matter coupling. Their energy-cumulant scaling is consistent with three-dimensional Ising and XY critical exponents, suggesting topological transitions in the $\mathbb Z_2$ and XY gauge universality classes, respectively. These results uncover additional phases generated by the competition between matter ordering and dynamical gauge frustration, whose physical characterization remains an open question.

</details>

*Alberto Brollo*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yang-Yang 的热力学 Bethe Ansatz（TBA）方法原本为量子系统发展，近年已成功推广到可积经典粒子系统，但从未应用于经典场论。现有经典推导未能一致地同时处理孤子、呼吸子、辐射（色散模）及其相互作用，因此需要一套能统一描述这些自由度的经典场论 TBA 框架。

Method: 作者直接从 Toda 链的逆散射变换（Inverse Scattering Transform, IST）出发推导 TBA 方程，得到孤子模与辐射模相互作用的描述，从而推广了纯孤子气体图像。随后将所得方程与已有的基于粒子模的成熟 TBA 方程进行数值对比验证。

Result: 成功导出了 Toda 链的 TBA 方程，其中包含孤子与辐射的相互作用，拓展了孤子气体图像；数值验证表明新方程与既有的粒子模 TBA 方程一致。

Conclusion: 该方法有望同样适用于 KdV 及纯辐射模型（如散焦 NLS 与 Sinh-Gordon）；同时指出在聚焦 NLS（及 Ablowitz-Ladik）和 Sine-Gordon 等模型中仍存在当前限制。

Abstract: The Thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz (TBA) procedure developed by Yang-Yang provides the free energy functional to be minimized via a variational problem. Their result not only gives the correct thermodynamics, but it also sets the framework for Generalized Hydrodynamics. Originally developed for quantum systems, this procedure has been recently successfully applied to (integrable) classical particle systems. However, this has never been done for classical field theories: no classical derivation has consistently accounted for solitons, breathers, radiation (dispersive modes), and their mutual interactions. We here derive the TBA equations for the Toda chain directly from its inverse scattering transform. This results in solitonic modes interacting with radiation, extending the soliton gas picture. We provide numerical verification of our equations against well established TBA equations in terms of particle modes. We expect the same procedure should apply to KdV, and pure radiative models as defocusing NLS and Sinh-Gordon, while we discuss current limitations in other models such as focusing NLS (and Ablowitz-Ladik) and Sine-Gordon.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文证明幂律弛豫满足最大熵准则，并据此预测有效频率范围随幂律指数增大而减小，该性质在部分生物材料中得到验证；同时建立了与分数阶标准线性固体的联系，并纳入拉伸指数行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幂律弛豫普遍存在于复杂力学系统中，通常被视为大量相互作用的指数响应的叠加结果。作者旨在探究这种普遍性背后的统计力学根源，即个体指数响应在部分信息（不完整知识）约束下究竟依何种准则被分配或调节。

Method: 采用最大熵准则（maximum entropy principle）对构成幂律响应的各个指数弛豫过程的贡献分布进行约束与推导，证明幂律响应满足最大熵条件；进而将结果与分数阶标准线性固体模型（fractional standard linear solid）关联，并扩展以涵盖拉伸指数（stretched exponential）弛豫行为。

Result: 推导出幂律响应可由最大熵准则导出，并给出一个可检验的预测：幂律的有效（适用）频率范围随幂律指数增大而减小。该预测在部分生物材料的实验数据中得到证实。

Conclusion: 幂律弛豫的普遍性可归因于最大熵原理这一信息论/统计力学机制；由此建立的统一框架将幂律弛豫、分数阶标准线性固体与拉伸指数行为联系为一体，为复杂力学系统提供了统一的力学谱学描述。

Abstract: The ubiquitous power-law relaxation emerges from complex mechanical systems with a vast number of interacting exponential responses. Here we demonstrate that power-law responses satisfy a maximum entropy criterion, regulating the individual contributions under partial knowledge. This leads to a prediction that frequency range of validity decreases with power-law exponent, a property which is confirmed for some biological materials. The close relation with the fractional standard linear solid also leads to an incorporation of the equally ubiquitous stretched exponential behavior.

</details>

Abstract: Target search processes are often carried out by diffusive elements that are introduced and removed at random times, causing fluctuations in the number of searchers in the system over time. For instance, proteins are synthesized and degraded stochastically in the cell, and susceptible to react with specific binding sites during their lifetime. Here we derive the density profile generated by non-interacting particles subject to stochastic synthesis and degradation (SSD) at constant rates, given a source location and an arbitrary propagator for the single, non-degrading particle. We also study the first-passage properties of these many-searcher processes, which can be deduced from the knowledge of the first-passage time distribution of a single particle. We analyse the behaviours of the characteristic reaction time and of the mean first-passage time in the limits of small and large rates. Taking into account the costs associated with synthesis leads to the existence of an optimal synthesis rate. We further generalise these results to bursty synthesis, when production events involve more than one particle at a time, as often observed in cell biology. The mean first-passage time in these situations can be significantly reduced at low synthesis or large degradation rates, as it is divided by the mean burst size in these regimes. The results presented here are applied to one-dimensional Brownian searches. Some general connections between SSD and resetting processes are pointed out as well as important differences, since SSD is associated with a distinct queueing protocol.

Result: 主要结果是普适类由尺度函数 s(x) 完全确定：共享同一 s(x) 的所有扩散过程具有相同的重正化轨迹统计，即使其微观轨迹完全不同。文中用无稳态与有稳态的多个过程（Cauchy、Student、Bessel、Fisher-Snedecor、几何布朗运动）阐述了全线与半线上最简单的普适类。

Abstract: For one-dimensional diffusion processes with arbitrary diffusion coefficient $D(x)$ and Ito force $F_I(x)$, we analyze the statistical properties of the renormalized trajectories at scale $Γ$, where one keeps only the alternating sequence of local minima and local maxima that are separated by drawups and drawdowns of amplitude bigger than $Γ$. The elementary RG-step between $Γ$ and $Γ+d Γ$ thus amounts to eliminate the drawups and drawdowns of amplitude $Γ$, a real-space-RG-procedure that is well-known for Brownian trajectories in the context of the Strong-Disorder-RG approach applied to the disorder landscape of random models. The main result is that the universality classes are determined by the scale function $s(x)$ satisfying $D(x) s''(x)+F_I(x) s'(x)=0$ that governs the exit probabilities from an interval : all the diffusions sharing the same scale function $s(x)$ have the same statistics of renormalized trajectories, even if their initial microscopic trajectories are completely different. The simplest universality classes for diffusions on the whole line $x \in ]-\infty,+\infty[$ and on the half-line $x \in ]0,+\infty[$ are illustrated with several examples of processes, with or without steady states: Cauchy, Student, Bessel, Fisher-Snedecor, Geometric Brownian. This framework concerning diffusion processes is then adapted to discrete-time and to continuous-time random walks on the one-dimensional lattice with arbitrary nearest-neighbor jump probabilities or rates.

Abstract: Strong anomalous diffusion is often characterized by a piecewise-linear spectrum of the moments of displacement. The spectrum is characterized by the slopes $θ$ and $ζ$ for small and large moments, respectively, and by the crossover moment $q_c$ at which the slope changes. The exponents $θ$ and $ζ$ characterize the asymptotic scaling of the bulk and of the tails of the probability distribution function of displacements, respectively. Here, we adopt asymptotic theory to match the behaviors at intermediate scales. The resulting constraint explains how distributions with algebraic tails imply strong anomalous diffusion, and it relates $q_c$ to the corresponding power law. Our theory provides universal hyper-scaling relations between the exponents characterizing strong anomalous diffusion, and it yields explicit expressions for the leading-order corrections to the asymptotic power-law behavior of the moments of displacement. We also characterize finite-time corrections and propose a robust distribution-based method to estimate $θ$, $ζ$, and $q_c$. The findings are supported by numerical and analytical results for three paradigmatic models exhibiting strong anomalous diffusion. Finally, we show that power-law tails are not only sufficient but also necessary for this scenario: with a Gaussian, or generally fast-decaying, bulk and exponentially rare ballistic excursions, the spectrum of moments is single-linear, and the crossover to the apparent second branch drifts with the observation time. When the bulk is fast decaying and the tail is a power law, strong anomalous diffusion is still present, but the hyper-scaling relations among the exponents are violated.

</details>

Conclusion: 弱耦合描述成功刻画了长程横场 Ising 链在短程交叉点附近的临界行为，解析展开与 DMRG 数值相互印证，证明了在 s=1.5 处交叉展开及在 s=1、1.25、1.5 处 Padé 插值的可靠性，为长程量子 Ising 模型的交叉区域提供了系统的理论框架。

Abstract: We propose a weakly coupled description of the long-range transverse-field Ising chain near its short-range crossover at $s_*=7/4$, where the couplings decay as $r^{-1-s}$. Abstract: We investigate quantum error correction protocols for topological codes deformed away from their stabilizer limits, particularly as they are pushed towards quantum phase transitions into trivial phases. Our focus is primarily on quantum information stored within the ground-state manifold of the transverse-field toric code (TFTC), subjected to independent bit-flip and phase-flip decoherence. Although the transverse field induces strong quantum fluctuations of $m$ anyons in the ground states, we show that projective measurements of the "bare" toric code stabilizers can nevertheless be used to construct an efficient decoding algorithm which exhibits a finite threshold to both types of decoherence, even when the transverse field is tuned infinitesimally close to the quantum critical point. Our construction of the optimal postmeasurement decoders---that is, the optimal decoders assuming that the stabilizers are first measured---crucially relies on the stoquastic nature of the TFTC Hamiltonian, which affords a description of its ground states in terms of a classical lattice gauge theory. Using stoquasticity, we first prove that stabilizer measurements act unitarily on the undecohered TFTC ground states to excellent approximation, and that the optimal postmeasurement decoder can be efficiently implemented via Monte Carlo simulation of a constrained lattice gauge theory. We then construct the optimal postmeasurement decoder for the TFTC states under bit-flip and phase-flip decoherence, where we numerically demonstrate a finite threshold close to the quantum critical point. We provide theoretical evidence (without a rigorous proof) that stabilizer measurements constitute an optimal decoding strategy in this setting. For phase-flip decoherence, we further prove that the optimal postmeasurement decoding threshold is independent of the transverse field strength throughout the topological phase.

</details>

Abstract: We examine the linear response of the $\PT$-broken phase of a BRST invariant model whose fermion masses form the complex conjugate pair $m_{\pm}=N\pm iav$. It splits into two channels, kept apart by an exact $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$, and one Euclidean correlator decides both. In the diagonal channel it oscillates in Euclidean time, so that channel admits no positive-norm realization: we exhibit a test function of negative norm and close every repair, the choice of boundary value among them. In the mixed channel the same correlator is completely monotone, which proves the spectral density positive; there we obtain the vacuum conductivity in closed form, with a real threshold at $ω=2N$, and positivity survives at finite temperature below a bound whose onset we give exactly. The longitudinal density of that channel is negative by the square of the mass splitting; an antisymmetric derivative removes it, and the resulting field-strength composite has a positive one-particle space and a causal commutator --- at the level of the two-point function only, since a four-point reflection form on the original composite comes out negative. The positive realization is therefore not a sector of the fermion model.

Abstract: The polarized IKKT matrix model is a $U(N)$ supersymmetric matrix integral proposed to be dual to an $N$ D-instanton sector of Euclidean type IIB string theory. We provide evidence that the physics of particular gravity saddles can be obtained through restricting the domain of the matrix model integral to a basin associated with the corresponding matrix model saddle. Abstract: We construct nonlinear electroweak clouds around Schwarzschild black holes in the bosonic Weinberg-Salam theory. These configurations are supported by harmonically time-dependent charged gauge fields, while their energy-momentum tensor remains static. Regularity at the horizon imposes a resonance condition relating the field frequency to the horizon electrostatic potential. Unlike linear clouds, these solutions exhibit a finite mass and electric charge gap, and exist only up to a maximal horizon size. We obtain both magnetic and electric clouds, including spherically symmetric and dipolar configurations, which reduce to electroweak balls in the vanishing-horizon limit. Abstract: Kormos, Runkel, and Watts (KRW) showed that a topological defect of a diagonal minimal model $M(p,p+1)$, perturbed by a weakly relevant defect field, flows to a new conformal interface. We study the flow of the $(2,1)$ Verlinde defect $D_{(2,1)}$ driven by the spinful defect field $φ_{(1,3)}\otimes\barφ_{(3,3)}$ on the lattice. The flow preserves the $(r,1)$ Verlinde lines. This preserved symmetry makes a dilute RSOS realisation possible. We study this flow numerically using both exact diagonalization and density matrix renormalization group (DMRG). At $p=4$, the flow ends on the invertible line $η=D_{(3,1)}$. At $p=5$ it ends on a non-trivial conformal interface $C_5$ with $g=1.503(2)$ and transmission coefficient $T=0.665(10)$. We report its spectrum, leading irrelevant dimension, and lattice $β$-function. Gauging $η$ maps $C_5$ to an interface of the three-state Potts model, which breaks $\mathbb{Z}_3$ but is a stable interface once the Fibonacci line $τ_{\mathrm{Fib}}$ and charge conjugation are imposed. A loop-model formulation allows us to follow the family $C_p$ at continuous $4\le p\le5$: it moves smoothly from $C_5$ to $η$ as we lower $p$.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Bit threads express holographic entropy as the maximal flux of a divergenceless vector field subject to a local capacity bound, but do not identify a physical carrier of that flux. In our previous work, we identified the projected string charge density with a bit-thread flow, reproduced the entropies of BTZ and ten-dimensional three-charge black holes, and showed that the maximal entropy flux is preserved under S- and T-dualities. However, the norm bound was imported from the bit-thread prescription, while the string-current configurations were prescribed along known bit-thread lines. In this paper, we give a stringy foundation for the local capacity and construct dynamical probe-string representatives of maximal flow. At genus zero and two-derivative order, the conical variation of the target-space action derived from the off-shell sphere fixes the string-frame entropy density. The gravitational replica construction and weighted flow-cut duality then yield the string-frame norm bound. We next find regular stationary F1 solutions in an NS-NS supported BTZ black hole whose spatial parts are logarithmic spirals. A uniform ensemble produces a conserved, constant-norm projected string charge density and realizes a maximal flow whose flux equals the BTZ entropy. We finally extend the construction to BTZ backgrounds with mixed NS-NS and RR flux. Abstract: Near-extremal black holes are controlled by a small set of soft modes, and counting these modes fixes the coefficient of $\log T$ in the entropy. The multiplicities stay the same, but the naive count fails. At fixed charge the Maxwell mode is frozen out, and the fifteen rotational modes of the $S^5$ act as a quantum rotor that only switches on above the scale $K_J^{-1}$. As a result, the coefficient of $\log T$ runs from $3/2$ at the lowest temperatures to $9$ above that scale, and never reaches the naive Gaussian value $19/2$. Result: 确定了显式分量形式的 counterterm，它内插两种不同的 old-minimal 超引力方案（具有不同的超共形反常）。counterterm 的一部分依赖于 old-minimal 辅助标量，构成文献 [1] 中超空间 counterterm 的分量形式；但完整的连接 counterterm 还包含额外项，这些项与 old-minimal 辅助标量无关，且在文献 [1] 的超空间 counterterm 中不存在。这些额外项在两个多重态之间物理可观测量的比较中起关键作用。

Conclusion: conformal 与 Ferrara-Zumino 多重态之间的联系由一个显式的局部 counterterm 给出，该 counterterm 超出文献 [1] 的纯超空间构造，其辅助标量无关的额外项对比较两个多重态的物理可观测量至关重要。

Abstract: A renormalized SCFT can be coupled to a number of background off-shell supergravity multiplets, defining different current multiplets and superconformal anomalies. We show that the conformal super-gravity partition function, when lifted to a functional of the old-minimal background fields through a suitable Wess-Zumino action, is related to the old-minimal partition function by a local counterterm that we determine explicitly in components. This counterterm interpolates between two different old-minimal supergravity schemes with different superconformal anomalies. Part of the counterterm depends on the old-minimal auxiliary scalar and provides the explicit component form of the super-space counterterm constructed in [1]. However, the full counterterm that interpolates between the two multiplets contains additional terms, independent of the old-minimal auxiliary scalar, which are not present in this superspace counterterm. These additional terms play a key role in the comparison of physical observables between the two multiplets.

### [96] [Weyl Rescalings: A First Order Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04978)
*L. Andrianopoli,J. Zanelli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文解决了一阶（Cartan）与二阶（度规）引力形式在Weyl变换下的明显不一致问题，指出关键在于考虑挠率的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weyl变换（度规的局域标度变换）被认为是极高能量下大自然的对称性，并在低能下（自发）破缺。在Cartan一阶形式中，标架按$e^a \to \Omega e^a$缩放，Lorentz联络$\omega$独立于标架，因此$\omega$与曲率$R(\omega)$必须是Weyl不变的；但在二阶度规形式中Ricci张量经历非齐次变换。两种物理上应等价（挠率为零极限下）的形式之间产生了矛盾。

Method: 作者将一阶与二阶引力形式的Weyl变换性质进行对比，通过引入并分析挠率（torsion）的作用来化解两种形式间的张力，从而阐明物质场与引力的共形耦合。

Result: 证明了一阶与二阶形式在Weyl变换下的矛盾可通过考虑挠率的作用加以解决，并借此澄清了物质场与引力的共形耦合问题。

Conclusion: 挠率是理解Weyl变换下一阶与二阶引力形式等价性的关键，这一分析为共形耦合提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Weyl transformations, --local rescalings in which the metric transforms as $g_{μν}(x)\longrightarrow Ω^2(x)\, g_{μν}(x)$--, are expected to be a symmetry of nature at extremely high energy, that should be (spontaneously) broken at low energy. In Cartan's first order formulation of gravity this translates into the condition that the local frames (vielbein) scale as $e^a(x) \longrightarrow Ω(x) e^a(x)$. In this construction, the Lorentz connection one-form fields $ω^a{}_b$ are independent from $e^a$, and since Lorentz transformations and dilatations commute, $ω$ and $R(ω)$ must be Weyl invariant, while in the second order metric formalism the Ricci tensor undergoes a non-homogeneous transformation. This generates a tension between the two formulations, which should be equivalent in the vanishing torsion limit. Here we show how the puzzle can be resolved taking into account the role of torsion. This also sheds some light on the conformal coupling of matter fields to gravity.

</details>


### [97] [Horizon exchange and the universal all-spin response of Kerr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05054)
*Pierre Vanhove*

TL;DR: 径向 Teukolsky 方程在内外视界交换下具有形式不变性，但物理边界条件并非如此。本文推导了对应 Mano–Suzuki–Takasugi (MST) 近区响应之间的精确单值（monodromy）关系。在频率最低阶、自旋任意阶下，该关系为每个多极矩给出了领头阶的“视界交换奇”响应表达式。对引力扰动，它确定了四极吸收中的交换奇贡献，并将交换偶动力学响应组织为 digamma 函数，其展开解释了 Kerr-Compton 计算中出现的奇、偶 zeta 结构。核心结果是该精确关系本身，而非此前已知的单个小自旋系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Teukolsky 方程在内外视界交换下具有对称性，但实际物理边界条件破坏这种对称性，导致视界交换对称性与非对称性（交换奇/偶）如何在可观测响应中体现尚不清晰。作者旨在从精确的 MST 单值关系出发，系统刻画交换奇与交换偶贡献，尤其是引力扰动中的低频动力学响应结构。

Method: 利用 MST（Mano–Suzuki–Takasugi）级数解框架，推导内外视界交换下近区响应之间的精确单值关系；在频率低阶展开到自旋任意阶，分离交换奇/偶部分，并用 digamma（polygamma）函数整理交换偶动力学响应，从而解析其 zeta 级数结构。

Result: 得到了精确的 Mst 近区响应单值关系；在频率最低阶、自旋任意阶下，为每个多极矩给出领头阶交换奇响应；对引力扰动确定四极吸收的交换奇贡献，并将交换偶动力学响应表达为 digamma 函数，其展开再现 Kerr-Compton 计算中的奇/偶 zeta 结构。

Conclusion: 精确的 MST 单值关系（而非此前已知的单个小自旋系数）是理解视界交换对称性破缺及引力扰动低频响应的核心，统一并推广了既有 Kerr-Compton 计算结果。

Abstract: The radial Teukolsky equation is invariant under exchange of the outer and inner horizons, although the physical boundary condition is not. At leading order in frequency and to all orders in spin, this relation yields, for every multipole, an expression for the leading horizon-exchange-odd response. For gravitational perturbations, it determines the exchange-odd contribution to quadrupolar absorption and organizes the exchange-even dynamical response into a digamma function whose expansion accounts for the odd- and even-zeta structures found in Kerr-Compton calculations. The exact relation, rather than the individual previously known small-spin coefficients, is the central result.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索曲率（Gauss–Bonnet）形变这种偏离纯AdS理论的高阶导数修正，如何改变探针模型相图中导电Weyl半金属相与绝缘flavour相的竞争，以揭示几何形变对相结构与输运性质的双重影响。

Abstract: We study how an effective Gauss--Bonnet deformation of the thermal geometry changes the competition between the conducting Weyl-semimetal and insulating flavour phases of the probe D3/D7 model. The phases are represented by horizon-reaching and smoothly capped D7 embeddings, respectively, whose free energies exhibit a first-order transition. At the temperatures examined, positive Gauss--Bonnet coupling shifts coexistence toward larger flavour mass and expands the thermodynamically preferred Weyl-semimetal region, while negative coupling shifts the boundary in the opposite direction. The normalized zero-temperature transition remains unchanged. An on-shell analysis shows that the leading finite-temperature shift is largely explained by an effective rescaling of the thermal scale, opposed by an intrinsic fixed-horizon geometric contribution. The enlargement of the conducting phase does not imply stronger electrical response: the longitudinal and anomalous Hall conductivity jumps at coexistence can decrease, and the coupling response of the longitudinal jump reverses with temperature. The mass-dependent maximum of the longitudinal DC conductivity can also cease to occur on the thermodynamically preferred branch. Thus the geometric deformation changes both which phase is favored and how the Weyl-semimetal phase conducts.

</details>


### [99] [From Z to all: cyclic sum rules for thermal CFT partition functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05281)
*Arnav Advant,Harsh Anand,Nathan Benjamin,Abhijit Gadde,Vipul Kumar,Shiraz Minwalla,Jyotirmoy Mukherjee,Sridip Pal,Asikur Rahaman,Pabitra Ray*

Abstract: The high-temperature expansion of twisted $S^{d-1}\times S^1$ partition functions of CFT$_d$, $d = 2κ$, has recently been shown to obey an infinite tower of universal constraints. We determine these constraints explicitly and demonstrate that they can be packaged into a single equation involving a large conformal diffeomorphism isomorphic to $\mathbb{Z}_{κ+1}$, which cyclically permutes the thermal circle and the rotation angles. Our equation asserts that the $\mathbb{Z}_{κ+1}$-invariant part of $\ln Z/β$ is a constant fixed by the a-anomaly. Our derivation assumes CP-invariance and locality of the thermal effective action, whose structure we constrain using an algebraic formalism starting from embedding space.

</details>
